- **The catalog text is built in English**, including every fixed word of a block (indica/sativa share, aroma, price per gram, the bulk line), and "Nothing to offer for now." when nothing qualifies. Its formats stay.
- **On screen**, Probarlo's and Buscar mi chat's reasons and the pin's statuses are English.
- **Posts already sent** are never re-sent, and there is no per-business language.
- **The pinned message** is edited in place into English by the existing upkeep, at the next trigger and at the latest at the next Argentina day change. Nothing new is built for this.
- A stored Spanish header and footer stay, so a catalog may mix languages.

**10. One place for the texts**
- Every front text is in one keyed module. No screen writes its own visible literal, and there is no library and no language switch.
- `LIMITED_COPY`, `LONG_REFUSALS` and `SAMPLES` keep running with no assertion loosened.

**11. A guard in the gate**
- **It fails when:**
  - a screen writes its own visible text;
  - Spanish appears in the keyed module, the API messages, or the catalog and bot texts.
- **It never flags:** seller data, the sheet's words, the kept formats or "Apostrophe".
- **It is** fast and date-independent, and it passes the real tree with no false alarm.
- A real offender is fixed, never exempted.
- How it detects Spanish and where it runs is the Tech Lead's decision.

**12. Docs, design and tests**
- The design-system README says English is the app's language and `pantallas/` are references for structure, not wording.
- `screens.json` follows the routes.
- The copy-keyed design guards, the capability docs' quoted copy, CONTEXT.md, the tests and the e2e follow the English app. No test is loosened.

No empty, loading or error state is new. Each existing one keeps its conditions and is shown in English.

#### Metrics

All of these are read from tests and the gate; there is no telemetry.

- **No Spanish (criterion 1).**
  - The guard passes over the real tree: 0 Spanish strings.
  - It is shown failing on a deliberately added Spanish message.
  - It is shown passing on seller data, the sheet's words and the kept formats.
- **One place (criterion 2).**
  - 0 screens with a visible literal of their own.
  - Changing one entry changes every screen that uses it.
  - Baseline: texts defined outside the module go to 0 at merge.
- **Nothing else (criterion 3).** Each of these is checked:
  - `contract.json` codes are unchanged;
  - formats are unchanged;
  - every old Spanish path redirects;
  - no route is Spanish in `screens.json` or the registry;
  - the manifest has `lang` `en`, `id` `"/"` and still passes installability;
  - the title and the document language are English.
- **Telegram.**
  - The catalog, its empty message included, is built in English.
  - Upkeep edits a published Spanish text once, in place, with no new post.
- **Seeds.**
  - A new business gets the English defaults.
  - Existing rows are untouched.
  - The import still matches and adds categories by name.
- **Limits.** The copy-limit tests pass on the English copy. The PR lists every shortened text and where its long form went.
- **Docs (criterion 4).** README, CONTEXT.md, `screens.json`, the capability docs, the tests and the e2e agree. The PR lists every edited test, and none is loosened.

### For the next role

For the Tech Lead:

1. **`docs/product/app-text.md` is the new capability file.** Until each other capability doc's quoted copy is updated, it overrides their "Rioplatense Spanish with vos" lines and Spanish quotations. Those updates belong to the implementation's diff.
2. **Update these in the same change:**
   - BRIEF.md's constraint, which becomes "Interface in English by default; code and docs in English";
   - every `_In the app_` line in CONTEXT.md;
   - the design-system README.
3. **The guard is yours to design.** You decide how it detects Spanish and where it runs. It must be fast, give the same verdict on any date and pass the real tree with no false alarm. It must never flag seller data, the sheet's words, the kept formats or "Apostrophe".
4. **Keep these fixed:**
   - refusal codes and `contract.json`;
   - the manifest's `id` `"/"`;
   - the import's accepted sheet words;
   - every format.
5. **No migration of stored rows.** Only the seed for a new business changes.
6. **The pinned message needs no new code.** It reaches English through the existing upkeep, at the next trigger or at the latest at the next day change.
7. **Tests follow the new texts.** None is loosened, and the PR lists every edited test and every shortened text.

## UX Designer

### Flow

GH-53 changes the words a person reads, not the steps they take. The PRD describes one job: read and use the app, its messages and its catalog in English. I write it below for each place the person meets text. In every one, the steps, screens, recovery paths, ending point and touch count are unchanged. Only the wording at each step changes. Routes, refusal codes, `contract.json`, formats (`$ 11.000`, `3,5 g`, `dd/mm`) and the tap budgets stay as they are. Old Spanish paths still redirect.

### Job: get in and reach Inicio (unchanged)

- Start: the person opens the app, or follows an old Spanish link.
- Steps: the login screen, then Inicio. Both screens now read in English.
- Old Spanish paths redirect to the English routes. That is the same redirect that exists today, not a new screen.
- What can go wrong: wrong credentials, a dead or expired link, or being offline. Each shows the same refusal, now worded in English. The recovery is the same: retry, or ask for a new link. These security texts reveal nothing they did not reveal before.
- End: Inicio, as before.
- Touches: unchanged. The page title and manifest `lang` are now `en`, which is not a step.

### Job: record a sale (unchanged)

- Start: Inicio. Steps and screens are the same as today. Labels, buttons, empty states and toasts are in English.
- What can go wrong: the sale is refused, for example for lack of stock, or the connection drops. The same refusal code returns, and the message from the API is now in English. The recovery path is the same: fix the line and retry.
- End: the sale is recorded, as before.
- Touches: unchanged, with the opening tap budget as defined in navigation.md.
- For UX Lead: English copy can run longer than the Spanish. The 80-character help-line limit and the 40-character empty-state title limit still apply. Anything over goes behind More info. This is a copy constraint, not a flow change.

### Job: collect payment (unchanged)

- Start: from the sale. The steps and screens are the same, and the cobro keeps its tap budget of at most 2 taps.
- Money keeps the `$ 11.000` format. Refusals and recovery are the same, and the texts are in English.
- End: payment recorded, as before.

### Job: check and adjust stock (unchanged)

- Start: Inicio, then the stock screen. The steps are the same.
- Weights keep the `3,5 g` format and dates keep `dd/mm`.
- Refusals and empty and loading states are in English. Recovery is the same.
- End and touch count: unchanged.

### Job: catalog, import and Telegram (unchanged)

- Start: the catalog screen, or the import screen. The steps and screens are the same, and the catalog keeps its tap budget of at most 2 taps.
- The pinned catalog message in Telegram is edited into English in place. The existing upkeep does this at its next trigger, or at the Argentina day change. Nothing is resent, so the person sees no new step or new message.
- Other bot posts and the operator command output are in English. What the person does with them is the same.
- Import errors keep the same codes and the same recovery (correct the file and re-import), now worded in English.
- End and touch count: unchanged.

### Job: settings and business setup (unchanged)

- Start: the settings screen. The steps are the same and the texts are in English.
- A new business is seeded with English categories, header and footer. A business that already exists keeps its stored rows, which are not touched. Its person can therefore see Spanish stored categories, header or footer next to English screens until they edit them. The edit path is the same as today. I flag this for UX Lead as an observation, not a new flow.

### Cross-cutting notes for UX Lead

- No screen, route, step or tap count is added or removed. No screen is invented.
- A screen that shows a literal not drawn from the single keyed text module is a defect against the PRD, not a different flow. The gate guard Tech Lead designed catches it.
- The prototype screens are in Spanish, and the new app-text.md capability file wins over the Spanish quotes. I have not redrawn any screen.

## UX Lead

_The system denied 1 write attempt: this role writes nothing; reproduce by reading the code, the task documents and the existing tests, or by running the declared checks. A denial here is not a syntax problem._

### Walk

I walked the prototype in `docs/product/design-system/pantallas`, starting at `Inicio.dc.html` and following its links. The prototype is entirely in Spanish, so I compared its structure and paths with the flow, not its words. The flow claims every step stays the same. Where the prototype could check that claim, it holds. Where it could not, I judged the flow's text.

**Get in and reach Inicio.** The path is Entrar.dc.html to Inicio. The dead-link state is "Eso ya no está." in Estados.dc.html. Invitacion.dc.html also stands in for Reset. Every step and every recovery the flow names exists, and only the words change. The old Spanish paths redirect, which is a routing fact and not a screen (sin prototipo). This job is consistent with the prototype.

**Record a sale.** The path is Inicio, then NuevaVenta, then NuevaVentaCliente, then "Venta guardada" in Estados. The steps match.
- English copy will run longer than the Spanish. The 80- and 40-character limits move overflow behind More info, which is not a flow change. It is worth checking by eye on the phone screens.

**Collect payment.** The flow says it starts "from the sale". VentaDetalle.dc.html has no link to Cobro. The prototype reaches Cobro.dc.html from Inicio's owes-you rows and from ClienteFicha. The job completes within its budget, but the flow names the wrong start point.

**Check and adjust stock.** The prototype has no "stock screen". The real path is Inicio's low-stock rows, or Variedades, then VariedadDetalle, then EntradaStock or TodasLasEntradas. Withdrawals go through SacarDelStock, which reuses EntradaStock's design. The job holds, but the flow's text does not match the prototype.

**Catalog, import and Telegram.**
- **Catalog:** Catalogo.dc.html links to VariedadDetalle#en-el-catalogo, ConfiguracionIntegraciones and ConfiguracionMensaje. The steps match.
- **Import (sin prototipo):** the flow starts from "the import screen". import.md says the import is an operator script, not a route or a screen, and that its report exists only on the terminal. The recovery (fix the file, run it again) holds, but the screen the flow names does not exist.
- **Telegram (sin prototipo):** the edit in place adds no step and no new message. The catalog may mix English fixed words with a stored Spanish header and footer.

**Settings and business setup.** The edit path for an existing business's Spanish seeds exists:
- ConfiguracionCategorias offers "Renombrá" for each category;
- ConfiguracionMensaje lets the seller edit the header and footer.

Nothing prompts the seller to change them, which matches the PRD's decision.

**Jobs the flow leaves out.** Their wording changes too, and nothing in them is blocked:
- Onboarding: NegocioNuevo, Invitacion and the Onboarding* screens;
- Expenses: Gastos and GastoNuevo;
- Análisis;
- Varieties and prices: Variedades, VariedadNueva, VerTodas and PrecioNuevo;
- Archived varieties.

**Cross-cutting.** The screens.json steps click and fill by exact Spanish text: "Entrá", "Contraseña", "Semana", "Elegir cliente", "Guardá la venta".
- Those steps must follow the English app.
- The `section` labels must stay the design's Spanish labels.
- From here on, comparisons check structure, not wording.

### Verdict

`approved`. Every job still completes in the prototype, with the same steps and recoveries. The findings are wrong start points (payment, stock, import) and missing jobs in the flow's text, not dead ends or missing recoveries.

## UI Designer

### Screens

General rule for every screen below: GH-53 is words only. Each screen is the product screen named, applied as is. Structure, hierarchy, typography, colours, spacing, components, routes, tap budgets, refusal codes and number/date formats (`$ 11.000`, `−$ 4.500`, `3,5 g`, `dd/mm`, catalog `$11.000`) do not change. The change is that every visible text comes in English from the one keyed app-text module. The `pantallas/*.dc.html` drawings are Spanish references for structure, not wording. A wording difference from a drawing is not a defect, and docs/app-text wins. Section labels stay as the design registry has them. Length limits still apply in English: help line or notice ≤80 characters, empty-state title ≤40. Where English runs longer, the short text stays and the how/why moves behind More info. Nothing is added, no icon is dropped, and the icon-and-text rule is untouched.

States listed per screen are the ones the screen already has. The PRD adds none, and each state just reads in English.

### Getting in: Entrar, Invitación (also used for password reset), NegocioNuevo
- Built from: Entrar, Invitacion, NegocioNuevo, unchanged.
- States: empty, filled, submitting, field error, brake (too many attempts), dead link (invalid or expired invitation/reset), success.
- Change: all labels, hints, buttons and errors in English. Entrar hint, brake message and dead-link texts must reveal nothing more than today (no hint about whether an account exists). Reset reuses the Invitación screen, with English text.
- Gap for UI Lead: there is no separate reset drawing. Confirm that reuse is acceptable.

### Inicio (phone, dark, compu): Inicio, InicioOscuro, InicioCompu
- Built from: the three Inicio variants, unchanged.
- States: first-run (empty), filled, loading, offline/notice, pending-cost notice.
- Change: greeting, day label ("Today 07/10", English weekday and month names), tappable notices, and the pending mark "and N g not yet costed" (fully pending: "N g not yet costed"). Notices stay ≤80 characters. If one doesn't fit, shorten it and put the detail behind More info.

### Nueva venta and Elegir cliente: NuevaVenta, NuevaVentaCompu, NuevaVentaCliente
- Built from: the same screens, unchanged.
- States: empty cart, filled, variety without price, customer picker (empty, search with no result, filled), saving, error, offline.
- Change: English labels, help lines and the empty-state title (≤40 characters). Refusal messages keep their codes and only the text changes.

### Ventas, VentaDetalle: Ventas, VentasCompu, VentaDetalle
- Built from: the same screens.
- States: empty, loading, filled, error. Detail has paid, owed and cancelled states.
- Change: English labels and statuses. Dates `dd/mm` and money formats stay as they are.

### Venta guardada and shared states: Estados
- Built from: Estados, unchanged (Venta guardada, Sin conexión, "That's gone" for Eso ya no está, confirmations, generic error).
- Change: English text. "That's gone" reveals nothing new about what existed.

### Clientes, ClienteFicha, Cobro: Clientes, ClienteFicha, Cobro, CobroCompu
- Built from: the same screens.
- States: empty, filled, search with no result, owed or settled, payment form (filled, error, saving, saved).
- Change: English words only. Cobro stays reachable from its existing starting points, namely Inicio, Clientes and ClienteFicha. This corrects UX Lead's walk, which put some starts elsewhere.

### Variedades, VariedadDetalle, VariedadArchivada, Archivadas, PrecioNuevo, EntradaStock, TodasLasEntradas, SacarDelStock
- Built from: the same screens.
- States: empty, filled, loading, archived, form error, saving, confirmation.
- Change: English labels, help lines and confirmations. Stock is reached through the existing variety screens, not from the Variedades list directly. This corrects the walk. "N g not yet costed" appears wherever cost is pending.

### Catálogo: Catalogo
- Built from: Catalogo, unchanged.
- States: filled, empty ("Nothing to offer for now."), loading, error.
- Change: English fixed words. The pinned message is edited in place by upkeep, with no new post.

### Configuración: Negocio, Usuarios, Precios, Integraciones, Mensaje, Categorías, Apariencia, TuCuenta
- Built from: the Configuracion* screens, unchanged.

### Onboarding: Onboarding*
- Built from: the Onboarding screens, unchanged.

### Gastos, GastoNuevo: Gastos, GastoNuevo
- Built from: the same screens.

I ran `scripts/design/compare.sh`. Every registered screen rendered with no failed step and no notes in `index.json`. None of these is blocking. No screen or state the PRD requires is missing, and everything written can be built from the product's own components.
