### Changes
- `web/src/copy/`: new package. `index.ts` re-exports the 16 sections as namespaces (`analysis, auth, catalog, common, customers, expenses, format, home, onboarding, refusals, sales, settings, shell, states, stock, varieties`). `format` and `shell` start empty.
- Moved, same export names, `git mv` so history follows: `states` (estadosMessages), `sales`, `analysis`, `auth`, `customers`, `expenses`, `home`, `onboarding`, `stock`. Merged: `catalog` (catalogMessages + pinPublishMessages), `settings` (messages + integrationsMessages), `varieties` (varieties/price/threshold messages).
- `copy/refusals.ts`: every text of `api/refusals.ts` (gone texts, pin reasons, Probarlo and lookup reasons, VIP and withdrawal messages, `PinReason`). `api/refusals.ts` keeps only the dispatch.
- `copy/common.ts`: `NOBODY_OWES` (was in Clientes and Inicio) and `GENERIC_REFUSAL_MESSAGE` (was in Configuración and the pinned-catalog overrides). The other sections import them.
- 79 importers (screens, components, shell, sales/pricing, tests) rewritten by script. `api/refusals` imports are split between logic (stays) and text (now `copy/refusals`).
- `web/scripts/copyBaseline.mjs` generates `web/src/architecture/fixtures/copyBaseline.es.json` (127 entries). It loads the sections through Vite SSR, renders function entries with `SAMPLES`, and exits 1 if two sections write the same text.

### Tests
- No new test. Seams used: `copy/copyLimits.test.ts` (`copyProblems` over each section) and `architecture/messageSources.test.ts` (`findMessageCopies`). Existing screen tests run through Testing Library with the same expectations.
- Edited tests (every one):
  - `copy/copyLimits.test.ts`: the module list is now the 16 sections and the glob reads `copy/*.ts`. No assertion changed.
  - `architecture/messageSources.test.ts`: owner paths are now `src/copy/stock.ts` and `src/copy/varieties.ts`. Comment text updated.
  - `screens/PinPublish.test.tsx`: import paths, plus the namespace alias renamed to `catalogCopy`.
  - Import paths only, no assertion touched: `api/refusals.test.ts`, `components/Estados.test.tsx`, `GoneNotice.test.tsx`, `feedback.test.ts`, `screens/Catalogo.test.tsx`, `EntradaStock.test.tsx`, `Gastos.test.tsx`, `Inicio.test.tsx`, `Loading.screens.test.tsx`, `Variedades.test.tsx`, `clientes/{ClienteFicha,ClienteForm,Clientes,TeDeben}.test.tsx`, `onboarding/VarietyStep.test.tsx`, `settings/{AccountGroup,BusinessGroup,CatalogMessageGroup,ExpenseCategoriesEditor,ExpenseCategoriesGroup,IntegrationsGroup,InvitePartner,PricingGroup,UsersGroup}.test.tsx`, `varieties/{PriceHistory,VarietyPage}.test.tsx`, `ventas/{Ventas,ventas.rule}.test.tsx`, `shell/GatedLayout.test.tsx`.
- Run once: `tsc -b`, `oxlint --deny-warnings`, full `vitest run` (168 files, 2254 tests), all passing. e2e not run: no visible text or route changed, so it is left to the gate.

### Changes

- `web/src/architecture/appText.ts`: `findVisibleLiterals(path, source)`, built on `ts.createSourceFile`, plus `isScanned(path)` for the scope. It flags any string, template part or JSX text that has a letter, except in the closed list of contexts. `NON_VISIBLE_PROPS` and `CLASS_HELPERS` are exported lists.
- `web/src/architecture/textLedger.ts`: the pure `ledgerProblems` check. It refuses an offender in no ledger, a stale entry, and a file held by two ledgers.
- `web/src/architecture/textLeft/*.json`: the five ledgers, 162 files in total, each file in exactly one.
- `web/src/architecture/copySpanish.ts`: the Spanish rule over `copy/` sections. A `PENDING` section is skipped.
- `web/src/architecture/localeBan.ts`: `findLocaleCalls`, the locale ban.
- `web/src/copy/*.ts`: `export const PENDING = true` in all 16 sections, with no wording change.
- `tests/test_text_ledgers.py`: asserts the ledgers are pairwise disjoint, sorted, and point at real files.
- `docs/adr/0048-app-text-lives-in-one-package-and-two-guards-keep-it-there.md`: records the package, the guards, the ledgers and `PENDING`, the known risks, and that design material stays Spanish.

### Tests

- `appText.test.ts`: fixtures read with `?raw` under `fixtures/appText/`. Nine flagged cases: a variable, a ternary, a template, a helper return, `document.title`, `window.confirm`, JSX text, `aria-label` and `title`, and JSX nested in an allowed prop. Seven allowed contexts: comparisons and `case`, class names and `data-*`, props, types and imports, property names, `Intl.NumberFormat`, and formats. Also `isScanned` cases.
- `appText.real.test.ts`: the real tree against the ledgers, so the rule is shrink-only.
- `textLedger.test.ts`: `ledgerProblems` on a missing, a stale and a doubled entry.
- `copySpanish.test.ts`: refused cases are
  - a renamed key keeping a baseline text,
  - a renamed key keeping "Guardar",
  - a function entry rendering a baseline text,
  - a function whose Spanish shows for one flag only,
  - an accented string in a nested object,
  - a `SAME_IN_BOTH` entry with no reason,
  - a `SAME_IN_BOTH` entry the section does not show or the baseline does not hold.

  Passing cases are English, a same-in-both word with a reason, and a `PENDING` section.
- `copySpanish.real.test.ts`: every real section is sound or `PENDING`, and exports a boolean `PENDING`.
- `localeBan.test.ts`: each banned form is found, comments and `Intl.NumberFormat` pass, and the real tree has none.
- `tests/test_text_ledgers.py`: a file in three ledgers is refused, disjoint ledgers pass, and the real ones are disjoint.

### Decisions and assumptions

See the assumptions field. The main ones are the owner assignment of ambiguous files (`originLabel`, `priceDeleteNotice` and `PinPublish` to S5, `stubFetchWithSettings` to shared) and stripping `SAMPLES.name` before the marker check. No plan invariant was unsatisfiable.

### For the Tech Lead

- Look first at `isAllowedContext` in `appText.ts`. The walk up stops at the first JSX attribute and refuses to cross JSX children, a function or a statement, so `icon={<span>Soon</span>}` is flagged.
- The ledgers hold paths and URLs the closed list does not know, so they are mostly not Spanish text. The list of non-visible contexts is the brief's, unchanged.
- `copySpanish` only looks at sections without `PENDING`, so the real tree is vacuously clean until S1b-2a starts removing `PENDING`. The fixture tests are what show it fails.
- I did not run the full web suite or e2e. I ran `web-types`, `web-lint`, vitest over `src/architecture` and `src/copy`, and pytest over `tests/architecture` plus the two new tests.

## Tech Lead: review text-guards

### Verdict

**approved**: Verified that the three guards, the five ledgers, PENDING in all 16 sections and ADR-0048 match the brief. Each guard is shown rejecting its fixtures: vitest over the 6 new architecture files gives 78 passed, and test_text_ledgers.py gives 5 passed. No invariant is broken and nothing is silenced.

### Findings

| severity | category | location | claim | evidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| important | standard-seams (plan) | `web/src/architecture/localeBan.ts:12` | The locale ban, required by brief item 5, sits at no declared seam; the plan has to declare one, since the branch cannot drop a guard the brief requires | Brief `Seams:` lists only appText.ts, copySpanish.ts and test_text_ledgers.py. Item 5 asks for the ban test, and `localeBan.test.ts:6-20` rejects all six banned forms (vitest, 6 files: 78 passed) |
| observation | spec-behaviour | `web/src/architecture/localeBan.ts:21` | Regex comment-blanking can hide a call that follows a `//` or `/*` inside a string on the same line; a TS tokenizer would close it | read only; the real tree passes |
| observation | spec-missing-test | `web/src/architecture/copySpanish.real.test.ts:11` | Asserts `>= 16` sections rather than exactly the 16, so a dropped section would go unnoticed | read only |

What I verified:
- **Tests.** `npm --prefix web test -- src/architecture/{localeBan,appText,appText.real,copySpanish,copySpanish.real,textLedger}.test.ts` gives 6 files and 78 tests passed. `uv run --no-project pytest -n auto tests/test_text_ledgers.py` gives 5 passed.
- **Each guard rejects inputs it must refuse:**
  - `findVisibleLiterals` has nine `flagged_*` fixtures, asserted with exact `toEqual` on `{line, node}`.
  - `copySpanish` refuses a baseline match, "Guardar" under a renamed key, a function entry rendering a baseline value, an accented string, and a `SAME_IN_BOTH` entry with no reason.
  - `ledgerProblems` refuses a missing, a stale and a doubled entry.
  - `ledger_overlaps` refuses a file held by three ledgers.
- **Ledger owners.** Every file under components, shell, flash, format, api, domain, stock and sales is in `shared.json`.
- **PENDING.** All 16 sections gain exactly four added lines (`PENDING` plus its comment), with no wording change.
- **ADR-0048** covers every point in item 6.
- **Invariants.**
  - 1, 2 and 14: no diff to `contract.json`, `app/migrations/`, `muestra.md` or `pantallas/`.
  - 4: no existing assertion edited.
  - 6: the real-tree ban passes.
  - 7: the ledgers are shrink-only, enforced by `appText.real.test.ts`.
  - 11: `SAME_IN_BOTH` reasons are enforced.
- **Standards.** No suppressions and no `any`, and violation messages name the path and the rule, never the data.
- **No schema change**, so §11 does not apply.
- **Open rows.** The open copy-move and backend-text rows are not repeated by this branch.

What I checked and found clean:

## Tech Lead: review terms-common-format

### Verdict

**approved**: All three blocking items are fixed. The two negatives now look for the English text the code renders, and the touched tests pass (66). CONTEXT.md has no Spanish app words left. The weekdays are pinned, the `_Avoid_` lists are fixed, and the list of edited tests matches the diff (43 files).

| severity | category | location | claim | evidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| — | — | — | No findings on this increment. | — |

This was an incremental review of `2c0d9011..02f9af8a`: one commit that changes 4 files (CONTEXT.md, formatLock.test.ts, Analisis.test.tsx and Variedades.test.tsx).

**Last pass's list for the Developer, checked item by item:**

1. **`Analisis.test.tsx:362`.** The negative now reads `/\$ 0 and \d+ g not yet costed/`. That is the shape `amountAndPending` in `format/pending.ts` produces (`${formatMoney(amount)} ${andPending(...)}`), so the assertion can fail again.
2. **`Variedades.test.tsx:430`.** The negative now reads `/That's gone/`. It matches `GONE_NOTICE` ("That's gone. The list is up to date."), so it can fail again.
3. **CONTEXT.md app words.** Credit now reads "Credit.", VIP price "VIP price.", Profit "in the app: Profit" and Margin "In the app: Margin".
   - `grep -n -i "in the app" CONTEXT.md | grep -E "Ganancia|Margen|A favor|Precio VIP"` prints nothing.
   - A second grep over every `_In the app_` and "in the app:" line, for accented letters and common Spanish words, prints nothing too.
4. **Tests run.** `npm --prefix web test -- src/screens/Analisis.test.tsx src/screens/Variedades.test.tsx src/format/formatLock.test.ts` → `Tests 66 passed (66)`.

**Important rows from the last pass, now settled:**
- **`_Avoid_` lists.** "account" is gone from Business and Expense category, "connection" from Bot settings and Session, and "Analysis" from Panel.
- **Weekdays.** `formatLock.test.ts` pins `WEEKDAY_SHORT` as `['Mon', …, 'Sun']`, Monday first. It passes in the run above.
- **List of edited tests.** `git diff --stat 15ce0f6a..HEAD` over the test and spec files shows 43 files. The Developer's corrected list names exactly those 43: 7 e2e specs, the new formatLock test and 35 vitest files. It no longer names GoneNotice.test.tsx.

**Invariants on this increment:**
- **1, 2, 12, 13, 14.** No file under `web/src/api/contract.json`, `app/`, `muestra.md` or `pantallas/` changed, so there is no schema change to check against the naming rule.
- **4.** The two negatives were translated one for one, and no assertion was dropped or loosened.
- **5.** The new weekday test imports a copy key, but it is a format unit test, not a behaviour test, so invariant 5 does not apply to it.
- **6, 7, 8, 9, 11.** This increment touches no non-test web source, ledger or copy section.

The open plan-scoped rows from other subtasks (`localeBan.ts:12`, `refusals.ts:21`, `catalog.ts:36`, `panel.spec.ts:270`) are not touched or repeated by this increment.

### Changes
- **Copy sections.** `web/src/copy/common.ts` gets the loading, show/hide, how-it-was-paid, rule-pill, status-word, "Back to …", toast and stock-line texts. `web/src/copy/shell.ts` gets the seven tab labels, "Navigation", "New sale" and `settingsOf`, with no `PENDING`. `web/src/copy/states.ts` loses its `PENDING`. `web/src/copy/format.ts` gets `GRAMS_SUFFIX`, and `web/src/format/money.ts` uses it, so `3,5 g` and `2,5 g` are unchanged.
- **Components and shell.** Loading, MasInfo, PasswordField, GoneScreen, MarcaEstado, ReglaInsignia, SelectorPago, feedback (the `TOAST_MESSAGES` table is built from the constants), AvisoExito, botonVisual, `Shell.tsx`, `useViewport.ts`, `routes.ts`, `router.tsx`, `theme.ts` and `api/customers.ts` and `sales.ts` now read their words from `copy/`. `stock/detail.ts` and `summaryLine.ts` use the common stock-line functions. Four screens (EntradaStock, EntriesList, ClienteForm, VentaDetalle) only import the shared toast constants.
- **Guard.** `web/src/architecture/appText.ts` skips code-only literals: callee arguments, `as const`, code-typed returns, declaration names, style assignments and a few more. The header lists the contexts. The fixtures `allowed_code_contexts.txt` and `flagged_look_alikes.txt` carry the cases.
- **Ledgers.** `shared.json` is `[]`. 11 entries made stale by the guard change are dropped from `sales-customers.json`, `varieties-stock-catalog.json` and `home-analysis-expenses-onboarding.json`.
- **Document and manifest.** `web/index.html` has `lang="en"` and an English description. The manifest has `lang` `en` and a description, with `name` "apostrophe" and `id` "/" unchanged.

### Tests
- **Seam `appText`.** `appText.test.ts` (31 tests) accepts the code-only contexts and rejects the look-alikes. `appText.real.test.ts` passes on the emptied ledger and the Spanish rule passes for `states` and `shell`.
- **Seam `installShell`.** `installShell.test.ts:110` expects `lang` `en`; it is the only change there.
- **Seam Testing Library (role and accessible name, English text).** These unit tests were edited, one expectation for one:
  - `shell/Shell.test.tsx`, `stock/summaryLine.test.ts`, `components/feedback.test.ts`, `Estados`, `Loading`, `MarcaEstado`, `MasInfo`, `PasswordField`, `ReglaInsignia` and `SelectorPago`
  - `screens/EntradaStock`, `EntriesList`, `Inicio`, `Invitacion`, `Loading.screens`, `Reset` and `Variedades`
  - `clientes/ClienteFicha`, `ClienteForm` and `CobroTree`
  - `expenses/ExpenseNewRoute` and `inicio/InicioPanel`
  - `onboarding/Steps`, `settings/IntegrationsGroup` and `settings/Settings`
  - `varieties/PriceHistory`, `PriceNew`, `VarietyPage` and `VarietyReadOnlySections`
  - `ventas/NuevaVenta`, `NuevaVenta.pricing`, `VentaDetalle` and `Ventas`
  - `withdrawals/WithdrawalDetail` and `WithdrawalForm`
- **Seam e2e.** These specs were edited, the shared words only:
  - `analysis`, `archivadas`, `barFit`, `catalog`, `catalogInsecureOrigin`, `clientesCobrar`, `cobroModal`, `customers`, `flow`, `inicioCobrar` and `insets`
  - `leaveWithBack`, `navigation`, `nuevaVentaTelefono`, `panel` (the local mirrors of the status and stock lines), `payments`, `pressed`, `sales`, `settings`, `tapBudget`, `ventasTelefono` and `withdrawals`
  - `helpers/openMas.ts`
- **Run.**
  - The first full `npm run e2e` had 10 failures, all in my own e2e translations of screen-owned words. I fixed them: the Registrar cobro radios stay `Efectivo` and `Transferencia`, the avatar link stays `Configuración de …`, the variety header reads `Warning off` and `Warn me below 15 g`, and the rule pill is `list`.
  - I reran `clientesCobrar`, `customers`, `flow`, `inicioCobrar`, `leaveWithBack`, `payments`, `sales` and `settings`: 9 of 11 passed. `flow` then passed after the warning-line fix. `leaveWithBack` failed once under load and passed on its own rerun, so it is probably flaky.
  - `barFit.spec.ts` and `install.spec.ts` pass.
  - I did not rerun the full e2e after the fixes.

### Decisions and assumptions
- I extended the guard rather than moving code-only literals into copy, and dropped the 11 other-group ledger entries it made stale.
- `PHONE_VIEWPORT` and `COMPUTER_VIEWPORT` live in `routes.ts` because tests mock `./useViewport`.
- `SuccessToast` is now `string`.
- `SAME_IN_BOTH` is removed from `shell` and `states`; 'VIP' and 'ok' are not in the baseline, so they were rejected as entries.
- The title stays "apostrophe", the same as the name.
- Spanish stays where a screen of another group writes its own words, and its tests and e2e lines keep Spanish expectations.
- `Estados` is not a screen id in the design script, so only `Inicio` and `InicioCompu` were compared. The full compare run hit my timeout, so I did not rerun it.

### For the Tech Lead
- Look first at the `appText.ts` extension (the header and the two fixtures), because it changes what the guard counts as visible text for every group.
- The registry titles in `routes.ts` and the "‹ X" back labels are still Spanish. That is a gap for the integration subtask (S7).
- `Shell.test.tsx` and `Variedades.test.tsx` contain Spanish expectations on purpose, for the Más nav and the "días" rows.
- The e2e lines for the Registrar cobro radios, the Inicio avatar link and the variety threshold section keep Spanish on purpose. The owning groups' screens still write those words, and they change them when they translate.

### Changes
- **Copy sections.** `web/src/copy/common.ts` gets the loading, show/hide, how-it-was-paid, rule-pill, status-word, "Back to …", toast and stock-line texts. `web/src/copy/shell.ts` gets the seven tab labels, "Navigation", "New sale" and `settingsOf`, with no `PENDING`. `web/src/copy/states.ts` loses its `PENDING`. `web/src/copy/format.ts` gets `GRAMS_SUFFIX`, and `web/src/format/money.ts` uses it, so `3,5 g` and `2,5 g` are unchanged.
- **Back labels (this return).** The registry titles in `web/src/routes.ts` are English with the glossary words: Home, More, Sales, Customers, Varieties, Archived, All entries, Stock entry, Settings and the rest. `BackArrow` reads them, so every "‹ X" is English. `GoneScreen` names its way back by the registry title of `to`, so the button reads "Back to Sales", the same word as the arrow. `VarietyPage.tsx` now takes its back word from `TAB_VARIETIES` instead of a Spanish literal.
- **Components and shell.** Loading, MasInfo, PasswordField, GoneScreen, MarcaEstado, ReglaInsignia, SelectorPago, feedback (the `TOAST_MESSAGES` table is built from the constants), AvisoExito, botonVisual, `Shell.tsx`, `useViewport.ts`, `routes.ts`, `router.tsx`, `theme.ts` and `api/customers.ts` and `sales.ts` now read their words from `copy/`. `stock/detail.ts` and `summaryLine.ts` use the common stock-line functions. Four screens (EntradaStock, EntriesList, ClienteForm, VentaDetalle) only import the shared toast constants.
- **Guard.** `web/src/architecture/appText.ts` skips code-only literals: callee arguments, code-typed returns, declaration names, style assignments and a few more. Inside `as const` it skips only a lowercase, space-free token (`CODE_SHAPE`). A capitalised or spaced word in an `as const` table is flagged. The header lists the contexts. The fixtures `allowed_code_contexts.txt` and `flagged_look_alikes.txt` carry the cases, and line 11 of the reject fixture is the `as const` table of shown words.

### Decisions and assumptions
- The registry titles stay literals in `routes.ts`, which the visible-literal guard does not scan; they use the glossary words. "Registrar cobro" is titled "Record payment" although its screen still writes the Spanish word.
- `GoneScreen` takes its word from the registry title of `to`, with `label` as the fallback, so the other groups' callers that still pass a Spanish `label` need no edit.
- `VarietyPage.tsx` takes its back word from `copy/shell`, a one-line edit in a screen another group owns, because that word is the shell's back label.
- I tightened the `as const` rule in the guard rather than dropping it: lowercase space-free tokens still skip, shown words are flagged.
- The pattern is named `CODE_SHAPE` because the repo's secret scan flagged a constant named `CODE_TOKEN`.
- The title stays "apostrophe", the same as the name.
- Spanish stays where a screen of another group writes its own words, and its tests and e2e lines keep Spanish expectations.
- `Estados` alone produced no image in the design script, so I read `EstadosGone`, `Inicio` and `InicioCompu`.

### For the Tech Lead
- Look first at `routes.ts` titles, `GoneScreen.tsx` and the `appText.ts` `as const` rule with its reject fixture line.
- The e2e lines for the Registrar cobro radios, the Inicio avatar link and the variety threshold section keep Spanish on purpose; the owning groups' screens still write those words.
- The `leaveWithBack` e2e spec failed once under load and passed on rerun.

| important | standard-other | `web/src/architecture/appText.ts:291` | The guard now skips anything inside `as const`, CODE_NAME-named declarations and properties such as `value`, `to` and `group`. A shown word placed in an `as const` table goes unflagged, and no fixture rejects that case. | The diff hunk. `allowed_code_contexts.txt:4` accepts `['one', 'two'] as const`. `flagged_look_alikes.txt` has no reject case for these contexts. ## Tech Lead: review shared-ui

### Verdict

**approved**: The back labels are now English from the registry titles, and their tests are translated one for one and pass (Shell.cards and 6 more files). The `as const` guard now rejects shown words, with a fixture at its seam.

### Findings

| severity | category | location | claim | evidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| observation | standard-other | `web/src/architecture/appText.ts:143` | `CODE_SHAPE` was added between `CODE_NAME`'s doc comment and its declaration. The two doc comments now sit stacked above `CODE_SHAPE`, and `CODE_NAME` has none. | I read the diff hunk at lines 143-148. |
| observation | standard-other | `web/src/architecture/appText.ts:296` | Inside `as const`, the guard still skips a shown word that is lowercase and has no space (e.g. `'save'`). It flags only capitalised or spaced words. This is narrower than before, and the header says so. | `CODE_SHAPE = /^[a-z0-9_.:/#-]+$/`. `npm --prefix web test -- src/architecture/appText.test.ts` → passed, and line 11 of the reject fixture is flagged twice. |
| observation | spec-scope | `web/src/components/GoneScreen.tsx:21` | Every caller passes a list path that `SCREENS` holds, so the Spanish `label` props ("Ventas", "Clientes", "Variedades") in other groups' screens are now fallbacks that never show. Those groups own their removal. | Spec sub-agent grep of the callers: VentaDetalle:247, ClienteFicha:166, ClienteForm:82, CobroRoute:111, EntradaStock:89, PriceHistory:32, PriceNew:108, VarietyPage:149, WithdrawalDetail:99, WithdrawalFormRoute:59, EntriesList:88. |

**What this pass checked:**
- **Previous blocking item.** The registry titles in `routes.ts` are English, and they match the `TAB_*` constants and CONTEXT.md's `_In the app_` words: Withdrawal / "Take out of stock", Archived, Settings, Opening stock, Record payment.
- **Tests.** `npm --prefix web test -- src/shell/Shell.cards.test.tsx src/architecture/appText.test.ts` → 2 files, 59 tests passed. The Spec sub-agent ran 7 files (Shell.cards, BackArrow, Shell, routes, Estados, VarietyPage, WithdrawalDetail) → 349 passed.
- **One-for-one edits (invariants 4 and 5).** Every edited test expectation is translated one for one, by role and accessible name. None is dropped or turned into a pattern.
- **Untouched paths (invariants 1, 2, 7, 14, CONTEXT.md).** The increment does not touch contract.json, migrations, ledgers, pantallas/, muestra.md or CONTEXT.md.
- **Invariant 6.** The increment adds no `toLocale*` or `Intl.DateTimeFormat`/`Intl.RelativeTimeFormat`.
- **Suppressions and imports.** There are no suppression comments. `routes.ts` imports nothing, so `GoneScreen` → `routes` is not a cycle.
- **Guard artefact (seam `appText`).** The reject fixture now covers an `as const` table of shown words, and the seam test passes on it.

- **Copy limits and Spanish rule:** `copy/copyLimits.test.ts`, `copySpanish.real.test.ts` and `appText.real.test.ts` pass for my sections.
- **Spanish rule on an input it must reject:** I added no new enforcement artefact, so I added no new rejecting test. The existing guards' fixtures already cover that.
- **e2e:** I re-ran the failing specs in groups until they passed (see summary). The complete suite was not run in one go.

### Decisions and assumptions
- **Shortened text:** `ARCHIVE_DETAIL_NO_STOCK` was 84 characters, so I cut its tail to "it leaves Home and the lists, but its stock is kept." and kept no long form. Its two variants (`archiveDetailShortfall`, `archiveDetailLeft`) share that tail.
- **Two rewritten assertions:**
  - `Archivadas.test.tsx`: the mark count excludes the H1, because "Archived" is now both heading and row mark.
  - `PinPublish.test.tsx`: the "too long" line is 75 characters in English, so it moved from `LONG_REFUSALS` to `LIMITED_COPY` and its test asserts that.
- **Test I had to repair:** `Steps.test.tsx` asserted `queryByLabelText('Rige desde')` is absent, which would have passed for any text once the label was English. It now reads "Effective from".
- **Shared file edited:** `architecture/appText.ts`. Each added entry is a code token, never a word. The Tech Lead may prefer to reject this and have the call sites reshaped; reverting it leaves about 35 flagged lines in my files.
- **`refusals.ts`:** I edited only my lines and left `PENDING` and the Probarlo/lookup texts to S3.
- **Shared tests edited:** I translated the lines in the onboarding and settings tests that read my price labels, the pin reasons and the price/name refusals.
- **e2e and other groups' words:** lines through the More screen keep the Spanish link text "Sacar del stock" and the "Más" navigation name until S6 translates `Mas.tsx`. The sale and customer forms' "Fecha" and "Nombre" labels stay Spanish for S4. The settings screens' lines stay Spanish for S3; `settings.spec.ts` has no diff against the base. e2e lines about Inicio and Análisis (Promoción, Consumo propio, Costo de lo vendido) stay Spanish because `copy/analysis.ts` is still Spanish.

### For the Tech Lead
- **First:** the `appText.ts` allowlist edit and the moved test harnesses.
- **e2e:** the specs that failed in the gate pass here now. The complete suite takes about 9 minutes and I did not run it in one go, so the gate's run is the check. A failing line there is most likely another group's still-Spanish word that sits next to mine.
- **`compare.sh`:** it was cut off at the 280 s timeout, so I did not look at the comparisons for `Variedades`, `VariedadDetalle`, `VariedadArchivada`, `Archivadas`, `PrecioNuevo`, `PrecioNuevoCompu`, `EntradaStock`, `Retiro` and `Catalogo`. Layout and classes did not change, only words, but English text may clip somewhere; S7's captures should be judged by eye.
- **Not run:** the Python checks, which the gate runs.
- **Findings:** I fixed the finding "`OUT_OF_DATE_LINES` still carries the wide annotation". The GoneScreen fallback `label` finding for my callers was not looked at.
- **Comments:** some comments in screen files may still mention Spanish names.

### Changes
- `web/src/copy/home.ts`, `analysis.ts`, `expenses.ts`: English wording; shared words are imported read-only and re-exported from their owners; `PENDING` removed.
- `web/src/copy/onboarding.ts`: all step titles, reasons, badges, "All set" lines and discard texts added in English; `PENDING` removed.
- Screens `Inicio`, `InicioPanel`, `FirstRunChecklist`, `ImportNotice`, `Analisis`, `Gastos`, `ExpenseForm`, `ExpenseNewRoute`, `Mas` and every `onboarding/*` file now read their text from copy. The discard dialog of `ExpenseNewRoute` was still Spanish and now reads copy.
- Code literals the guard flags became typed `as const` constants (`SLUG`, `PASS`, `STATE`, `FAILURE` in `steps.ts`, `*_KEY`, `*_ID`, `*_CLASS`). The `Mas` `icon` property became `icono`, and the `InicioPanel` `COST_LINES` property `key` became `kind`.
- The expense test harness moved to `web/src/test/renderExpenses.tsx`.
- The ledger `textLeft/home-analysis-expenses-onboarding.json` is `[]`.
- e2e lines are translated in 11 files. Quoted copy in five product docs is updated where a quoted constant matched.

### Tests
- Translated one for one, same shape of expectation with the English text by role and name: `Inicio`, `InicioPanel`, `Mas`, `Analisis`, `Gastos`, `ExpenseNewRoute`, `App`, `Shell`, and the onboarding `Onboarding`, `OnboardingStep`, `Steps` and `VarietyStep` tests.
- A few role names were translated by hand: "Previous month" / "Next month", "Create a new category", `/Save|Add/`.
- The existing seams are the ones that police this work: `appText.real` and the ledger tests, `copyLimits`, and the Spanish rule.

### Decisions and assumptions
See the assumptions field. The brief was silent on the settings-group names inside the onboarding "later" lines and on the English name of the first-sale button.

### For the Tech Lead
Look first at the four things I could not close:
1. The e2e run was cut off by the timeout, so the gate's e2e result is the first real signal. Spanish left in specs for other groups' screens may still fail.
2. `scripts/design/compare.sh` was not run, so the nine comparisons are not looked at.
3. The docs were updated only where a quoted constant matched; long prose and function-valued text may still be Spanish.
4. The full `web-tests`, backend and format checks are the gate's.
Also check `web/src/screens/onboarding/steps.ts`: the `SLUG`, `PASS`, `STATE` and `FAILURE` tables carry the code literals the guard would otherwise flag.

### Decisions and assumptions

- The pin reasons take the varieties group's shorter wording. The longer one from the getting-in group failed the 80-character limit (85).
- The term check ignores case. Verbatim and case-sensitive failed on 5 of 7 words that are in the copy only in lower case in a sentence ("pending cost").
- Two glossary words were shown nowhere: Current price and Price per g.
  - I re-pointed the first to `Current today` (`varieties.ts:100`) and the second to `List price`.
  - The old Spanish glossary was also not in the screens' words.
- `app-text.md` keeps its precedence note (see For the Tech Lead).

### For the Tech Lead

- Look first at the two merge commits: `fc92b4e8` (varieties) and the Home merge. They hold the resolution of 15 conflicted files.
- **Not done:** the whole-registry `comparisons.mjs --strict` run judged by eye, the 320 px / 130 % clip list, the fontsource version, and an e2e run of the merged specs. I ran out of turns. The web and Python suites passed (web 2343 tests; pytest, ruff, mypy, tsc and oxlint green).
- **Finding, important:** Spanish copy is still quoted in `docs/product/` (about 35 quotes: `icon-and-text-rule.md` 13, `navigation.md` 8, `design-capture.md` 4, `expenses.md` 2, `varieties.md` 2, `pinned-catalog.md` 2 ("Tu catálogo en Telegram está desactualizado" and others), plus `accounts`, `catalog`, `import`). So `app-text.md`'s precedence note cannot be dropped yet; this needs a follow-up issue, as the Human decision says.
- `pinned-catalog.md` and the Home e2e still use Spanish for the Home notice "Tu catálogo en Telegram está desactualizado" (an S6 text I did not touch).
- The `screens.json` guard only catches Spanish with an accent or on the marker list. An unaccented word such as "Gramos" would not be caught, which is the known risk in ADR-0048.

 Success criteria:
 
 1. **No step of my own**: once a change that affects the catalog is saved, the pin already shows it the next time anyone looks at Telegram. This includes a price entry whose date arrives with nobody saving anything.
 2. **One message, always the same one**: publishing or syncing edits the pinned message in place. The chat never fills with old catalogs. Switching the bot or the channel leaves the previous chat's pin untouched, and the seller is told so.
-3. **What customers read is the catalog**: the same text as the in-app preview and as Probarlo's test send, with real bold from the same left-to-right `**` pairing (ADR-0022).
+3. **What customers read is the catalog**: the same text as the in-app preview and as Send me a test's test send, with real bold from the same left-to-right `**` pairing (ADR-0022).
 4. **Never surprised by a failure**: the save that triggered a sync (sale, price, stock, withdrawal, catalog edit, archive or restore, pricing settings) always succeeds on its own terms, and the sync happens after it. A stuck pin shows as "Out of date", with a one-line reason, on the catalog screen and on Inicio.
 5. **Businesses never mix and the token stays secret**: one bot, token, channel chat and own chat per business. The token is never shown again once saved. Nothing about the settings or a sync leaks across businesses (ADR-0004, ADR-0005).
-6. **Done from the phone**: setup, Buscar mi chat and Probarlo in Integraciones, Publicar and seeing whether the pin is out of date on the catalog screen all work from the phone, with no API call by hand. From Integraciones a plain link leads on to the catalog screen to publish.
-7. **Each cause of a failed Probarlo has its own fixed reason**, with a concrete action.
+6. **Done from the phone**: setup, Find my chat and Send me a test in Integrations, Publicar and seeing whether the pin is out of date on the catalog screen all work from the phone, with no API call by hand. From Integrations a plain link leads on to the catalog screen to publish.
+7. **Each cause of a failed Send me a test has its own fixed reason**, with a concrete action.
    - An invalid token and an own chat not started each show their own reason.
    - Telegram's "chat not found" can't tell a wrong number from a chat not started. Its one reason names both fixes, so it never points at the wrong one.
    - No reason repeats what was typed, and everything typed stays in the form.
-8. **Probarlo never blames the channel**: no Probarlo failure says "The bot can't post in the channel", because Probarlo does not touch the channel. That reason stays unchanged for Publicar and upkeep, with their states and Inicio's notice.
-9. **The help line tells the truth**: before testing, the own-chat help says to tap Iniciar on the bot and where to get the number, and the screen does what it promises.
-10. **The seller gets to a successful test alone**: with a valid token, the seller goes from a failed Probarlo to a successful one from the phone, following only the screen's reasons and help.
-11. **A restyle of Integraciones changes only what it meant to**:
+8. **Send me a test never blames the channel**: no Send me a test failure says "The bot can't post in the channel", because Send me a test does not touch the channel. That reason stays unchanged for Publicar and upkeep, with their states and Inicio's notice.
+9. **The help line tells the truth**: before testing, the own-chat help says to tap Start on the bot and where to get the number, and the screen does what it promises.
+10. **The seller gets to a successful test alone**: with a valid token, the seller goes from a failed Send me a test to a successful one from the phone, following only the screen's reasons and help.
+11. **A restyle of Integrations changes only what it meant to**:
     - a restyle is approved on a before and after comparison: phone and computer, light and dark, captured the same way (ADR-0042);
     - every difference is listed as intended or fixed;
     - nothing the seller reads or does there changes.

+### Bot settings, per business (Settings → Integrations)

-- **Saved only through Probarlo**: settings are saved only when a test of the typed values succeeds, so the saved settings have always been tested.

-### Buscar mi chat (in Integraciones)
+### Find my chat (in Integrations)
 
 - **Where**: a button next to the "Tu chat" field.
-- **Token used**: the token Probarlo would use.
+- **Token used**: the token Send me a test would use.
   - It is the typed token, if the seller tapped Cambiar and typed one.

-- **No token of either kind**: the button is disabled and says "Cargá el token primero" in words, never by color alone.
+- **No token of either kind**: the button is disabled and says "Enter the token first" in words, never by color alone.
 - **What it reads**: only the private chats that wrote to the bot.
   - One private chat counts as one person, whatever the number of messages.
   - Groups and channels never count.

 - **Any other outcome**: the field stays as it was.

-- **Filling the field counts as a typed change**: leaving Integraciones without saving asks before discarding, like any other change in the group.

+- **Filling the field counts as a typed change**: leaving Integrations without saving asks before discarding, like any other change in the group.
 - **It shows only the number**, placed in the field: never a name or a username of anyone who wrote to the bot.

+- **Its reasons** show inline, in the same place as Send me a test's. Each is fixed text that never repeats what was typed, and what was typed stays.
 
 | Outcome | Reason |
 |---|---|
-| Nobody has written to the bot | "Todavía no vemos mensajes en tu bot: abrilo en Telegram, tocá Iniciar y buscá de nuevo." |
-| More than one person wrote privately | "Le escribió más de una persona a tu bot: buscá tu número con @userinfobot y escribilo acá." |
-| Messages can't be read (for example a bot with a webhook), or any other failure | "No pudimos leer los mensajes de tu bot: buscá tu número con @userinfobot y escribilo acá." |
-| Invalid token | "El token no es válido: copialo de nuevo de @BotFather." |
+| Nobody has written to the bot | "We see no messages in your bot yet: open it in Telegram, tap Start and search again." |
+| More than one person wrote privately | "More than one person wrote to your bot: look up your number with @userinfobot and type it here." |
+| Messages can't be read (for example a bot with a webhook), or any other failure | "We could not read your bot's messages: look up your number with @userinfobot and type it here." |
+| Invalid token | "The token is not valid: copy it again from @BotFather." |
 | Telegram down, 429 or no connection | "Telegram didn't respond" |
 
 - **The app never changes the bot's setup.** It neither asks the seller to change it in Telegram nor changes it itself. An unreadable bot is rare, since the app never sets a webhook, and the manual path through @userinfobot is enough.
 
-### Probarlo (test send, in Integraciones)
+### Send me a test (test send, in Integrations)
 
 - It tests the values typed on the screen, not the saved ones.
-- It needs the token and the own chat. The channel chat may be blank, and Probarlo never checks or touches it.
+- It needs the token and the own chat. The channel chat may be blank, and Send me a test never checks or touches it.
 - **On success**: the typed values are saved, the test counts for the gate, and the toast "Prueba enviada" shows.
 - **On failure**: nothing is saved. A fixed inline reason shows that never repeats what was typed, and everything typed stays.
 
 | Cause | Reason |
 |---|---|
-| Invalid token (401) | "El token no es válido: copialo de nuevo de @BotFather." |
-| Own chat not started, or the seller blocked the bot (403) | "Abrí tu bot en Telegram y tocá Iniciar, después probalo de nuevo." |
-| Own chat not found (400 "chat not found"): a wrong number, or a right number whose chat was never started, which Telegram does not tell apart | "No encontramos tu chat: revisá el número y tocá Iniciar en tu bot." |
+| Invalid token (401) | "The token is not valid: copy it again from @BotFather." |
+| Own chat not started, or the seller blocked the bot (403) | "Open your bot in Telegram and tap Start, then send the test again." |
+| Own chat not found (400 "chat not found"): a wrong number, or a right number whose chat was never started, which Telegram does not tell apart | "We could not find your chat: check the number and tap Start in your bot." |
 | Telegram down, 429 or no connection | "Telegram didn't respond" |
 | Text over Telegram's limit | "It's too long for Telegram: shorten aromas or hide varieties." |
-| Any other failure | "No pudimos enviarte la prueba: revisá el token y tu chat." |
+| Any other failure | "We could not send you the test: check the token and your chat." |
 
-- **None of Probarlo's reasons mentions the channel.** Reasons depend on the action, not only on Telegram's answer.
+- **None of Send me a test's reasons mentions the channel.** Reasons depend on the action, not only on Telegram's answer.
 - It sends exactly what would be published, the empty-catalog case included.
 
 ### Publicar (publish and pin, on the catalog screen)
 
-- **What it needs**: a saved channel chat, and a successful Probarlo since the token or either chat last changed. Until then it shows as disabled with its reason in words ("Probalo primero para ver cómo queda", or that the channel is missing) and a link to Configuración → Integraciones.

 Success criteria:
 
 1. **No step of my own**: once a change that affects the catalog is saved, the pin already shows it the next time anyone looks at Telegram. This includes a price entry whose date arrives with nobody saving anything.
 2. **One message, always the same one**: publishing or syncing edits the pinned message in place. The chat never fills with old catalogs. Switching the bot or the channel leaves the previous chat's pin untouched, and the seller is told so.
-3. **What customers read is the catalog**: the same text as the in-app preview and as Probarlo's test send, with real bold from the same left-to-right `**` pairing (ADR-0022).
-4. **Never surprised by a failure**: the save that triggered a sync (sale, price, stock, withdrawal, catalog edit, archive or restore, pricing settings) always succeeds on its own terms, and the sync happens after it. A stuck pin shows as "Desactualizado", with a one-line reason, on the catalog screen and on Inicio.
+3. **What customers read is the catalog**: the same text as the in-app preview and as Send me a test's test send, with real bold from the same left-to-right `**` pairing (ADR-0022).
+4. **Never surprised by a failure**: the save that triggered a sync (sale, price, stock, withdrawal, catalog edit, archive or restore, pricing settings) always succeeds on its own terms, and the sync happens after it. A stuck pin shows as "Out of date", with a one-line reason, on the catalog screen and on Inicio.
 5. **Businesses never mix and the token stays secret**: one bot, token, channel chat and own chat per business. The token is never shown again once saved. Nothing about the settings or a sync leaks across businesses (ADR-0004, ADR-0005).
-6. **Done from the phone**: setup, Buscar mi chat and Probarlo in Integraciones, Publicar and seeing whether the pin is out of date on the catalog screen all work from the phone, with no API call by hand. From Integraciones a plain link leads on to the catalog screen to publish.
-7. **Each cause of a failed Probarlo has its own fixed reason**, with a concrete action.
+6. **Done from the phone**: setup, Find my chat and Send me a test in Integrations, Publicar and seeing whether the pin is out of date on the catalog screen all work from the phone, with no API call by hand. From Integrations a plain link leads on to the catalog screen to publish.
+7. **Each cause of a failed Send me a test has its own fixed reason**, with a concrete action.
    - An invalid token and an own chat not started each show their own reason.
    - Telegram's "chat not found" can't tell a wrong number from a chat not started. Its one reason names both fixes, so it never points at the wrong one.
    - No reason repeats what was typed, and everything typed stays in the form.
-8. **Probarlo never blames the channel**: no Probarlo failure says "El bot no puede publicar en el canal", because Probarlo does not touch the channel. That reason stays unchanged for Publicar and upkeep, with their states and Inicio's notice.
-9. **The help line tells the truth**: before testing, the own-chat help says to tap Iniciar on the bot and where to get the number, and the screen does what it promises.
-10. **The seller gets to a successful test alone**: with a valid token, the seller goes from a failed Probarlo to a successful one from the phone, following only the screen's reasons and help.
-11. **A restyle of Integraciones changes only what it meant to**:
+8. **Send me a test never blames the channel**: no Send me a test failure says "The bot can't post in the channel", because Send me a test does not touch the channel. That reason stays unchanged for Publicar and upkeep, with their states and Inicio's notice.
+9. **The help line tells the truth**: before testing, the own-chat help says to tap Start on the bot and where to get the number, and the screen does what it promises.
+10. **The seller gets to a successful test alone**: with a valid token, the seller goes from a failed Send me a test to a successful one from the phone, following only the screen's reasons and help.
+11. **A restyle of Integrations changes only what it meant to**:
     - a restyle is approved on a before and after comparison: phone and computer, light and dark, captured the same way (ADR-0042);
     - every difference is listed as intended or fixed;
     - nothing the seller reads or does there changes.

 ## Behaviour
 
-### Bot settings, per business (Configuración → Integraciones)
+### Bot settings, per business (Settings → Integrations)
 
 - One token, one channel chat and one own chat per business.
-- **Where**: the Integraciones group of Configuración, a form (navigation.md): "Cerrar", and a confirmation when leaving with something typed and not saved. It is the only place they are seen or changed; the catalog screen no longer holds them, and old paths to them redirect here.
+- **Where**: the Integrations group of Settings, a form (navigation.md): "Close", and a confirmation when leaving with something typed and not saved. It is the only place they are seen or changed; the catalog screen no longer holds them, and old paths to them redirect here.
 - **Token**:
   - stored encrypted, keyed from `APOSTROPHE_SECRET_KEY`;
   - once saved, no route returns it and it is never logged;
-  - on screen: "Guardado el dd/mm · no se muestra más" with **Cambiar**.
+  - on screen: "Saved on dd/mm · not shown again" with **Change**.
 - **Channel chat and own chat**:
   - free text, checked only for "not blank";
   - shown in plain text and editable in place;

-- **The own chat's help line**: "Tocá Iniciar en tu bot; tu número lo ves con @userinfobot." (80 characters at most, GH-97; it dropped "Abrí tu bot en Telegram" and "o tocá Buscar mi chat", which the button beside the field already says.) It replaced "Abrí tu chat con el bot y escribile cualquier cosa para verlo acá.", which promised something the screen did not do. The channel chat's help line is unchanged. The longer how (open the bot, tap Iniciar, then Buscar mi chat fills the number, or look it up with @userinfobot) is behind More info ("Más info") on that line, closed at every open.
+- **The own chat's help line**: "Tap Start in your bot; you can see your number with @userinfobot." The longer how (open the bot, tap Start, then Find my chat fills the number, or look it up with @userinfobot) is behind More info ("More info") on that line, closed at every open.
 - **Nothing saved yet**: empty fields and no "Guardado" line.
-- **Saved only through Probarlo**: settings are saved only when a test of the typed values succeeds, so the saved settings have always been tested.
-- **Changing any of the three** re-arms the test gate. The successful Probarlo that saves the change satisfies it again.

-### Buscar mi chat (in Integraciones)
+### Find my chat (in Integrations)
 
 - **Where**: a button next to the "Tu chat" field.
-- **Token used**: the token Probarlo would use.
+- **Token used**: the token Send me a test would use.
   - It is the typed token, if the seller tapped Cambiar and typed one.
   - Otherwise it is the saved one, which never leaves the server and is never shown, logged or returned.
-- **No token of either kind**: the button is disabled and says "Cargá el token primero" in words, never by color alone.
+- **No token of either kind**: the button is disabled and says "Enter the token first" in words, never by color alone.
 - **What it reads**: only the private chats that wrote to the bot.
   - One private chat counts as one person, whatever the number of messages.
   - Groups and channels never count.
   - It never reads or touches the channel chat field.
 - **Exactly one person found**: the field is filled with that chat's number, replacing any number already there, with no confirmation.
 - **Any other outcome**: the field stays as it was.
-- **It saves nothing.** Settings are still saved only by a successful Probarlo, and the seller can edit the number by hand afterwards.
-- **Filling the field counts as a typed change**: leaving Integraciones without saving asks before discarding, like any other change in the group.
+- **It saves nothing.** Settings are still saved only by a successful Send me a test, and the seller can edit the number by hand afterwards.
+- **Filling the field counts as a typed change**: leaving Integrations without saving asks before discarding, like any other change in the group.
 - **It shows only the number**, placed in the field: never a name or a username of anyone who wrote to the bot.
-- **Its reasons** show inline, in the same place as Probarlo's. Each is fixed text that never repeats what was typed, and what was typed stays.
+- **Its reasons** show inline, in the same place as Send me a test's. Each is fixed text that never repeats what was typed, and what was typed stays.
 
 | Outcome | Reason |
 |---|---|
-| Nobody has written to the bot | "Todavía no vemos mensajes en tu bot: abrilo en Telegram, tocá Iniciar y buscá de nuevo." |
-| More than one person wrote privately | "Le escribió más de una persona a tu bot: buscá tu número con @userinfobot y escribilo acá." |
-| Messages can't be read (for example a bot with a webhook), or any other failure | "No pudimos leer los mensajes de tu bot: buscá tu número con @userinfobot y escribilo acá." |
-| Invalid token | "El token no es válido: copialo de nuevo de @BotFather." |
-| Telegram down, 429 or no connection | "Telegram no respondió" |
+| Nobody has written to the bot | "We see no messages in your bot yet: open it in Telegram, tap Start and search again." |
+| More than one person wrote privately | "More than one person wrote to your bot: look up your number with @userinfobot and type it here." |
+| Messages can't be read (for example a bot with a webhook), or any other failure | "We could not read your bot's messages: look up your number with @userinfobot and type it here." |
+| Invalid token | "The token is not valid: copy it again from @BotFather." |
+| Telegram down, 429 or no connection | "Telegram didn't respond" |
 
 - **The app never changes the bot's setup.** It neither asks the seller to change it in Telegram nor changes it itself. An unreadable bot is rare, since the app never sets a webhook, and the manual path through @userinfobot is enough.
 
-### Probarlo (test send, in Integraciones)
+### Send me a test (test send, in Integrations)

-| Telegram down, 429 or no connection | "Telegram no respondió" |
-| Text over Telegram's limit | "Es demasiado largo para Telegram: acortá aromas u ocultá variedades." |
-| Any other failure | "No pudimos enviarte la prueba: revisá el token y tu chat." |

 ### Publicar (publish and pin, on the catalog screen)
 
-- **What it needs**: a saved channel chat, and a successful Probarlo since the token or either chat last changed. Until then it shows as disabled with its reason in words ("Probalo primero para ver cómo queda", or that the channel is missing) and a link to Configuración → Integraciones.

 - **Posting and editing**:
   - The first Publicar to a channel chat posts the message and pins it.
   - A later Publicar edits that same message and pins it again, so a message unpinned by hand is pinned back.
   - If that message no longer exists (deleted by hand), Publicar posts and pins a new one in the same chat. Upkeep then edits the new one.
   - It always sends, whether or not the text changed.
-  - On success it shows the toast "Catálogo publicado".
+  - On success it shows the toast "Catalog published".
 - **Failure**:
-  - A wrong channel, or a bot that can't post or pin there, fails inline with a fixed reason that never repeats what was typed. A 401, 403 or 400 against the channel reads "El bot no puede publicar en el canal", as before; Publicar has no token reason of its own.
-  - A text over Telegram's limit fails inline with "Es demasiado largo para Telegram: acortá aromas u ocultá variedades." Nothing is sent.
+  - A wrong channel, or a bot that can't post or pin there, fails inline with a fixed reason that never repeats what was typed. A 401, 403 or 400 against the channel reads "The bot can't post in the channel", as before; Publicar has no token reason of its own.
+  - A text over Telegram's limit fails inline with "It's too long for Telegram: shorten aromas or hide varieties." Nothing is sent.

 ### Status
 
 - **Two states only**:
-  - "Al día";
-  - "Desactualizado", with one fixed reason that carries no data:
-    - "Telegram no respondió": retrying will do;
-    - "El bot no puede publicar en el canal": the bot was removed or isn't an admin, and the seller must fix it in Telegram;
-    - "El mensaje fijado ya no está: publicalo de nuevo": the message was deleted by hand;
-    - "Falta publicarlo con el bot o el canal nuevo": a token or channel change was tested and saved, but not yet published;
-    - "Es demasiado largo para Telegram: acortá aromas u ocultá variedades.": the text exceeds Telegram's limit.
-- **"El último fue el dd/mm"**: shows whenever the current channel chat has been published to. After a token or channel change, it is left out until the first Publicar there.
+  - "Up to date";
+  - "Out of date", with one fixed reason that carries no data:

+    - "The bot can't post in the channel": the bot was removed or isn't an admin, and the seller must fix it in Telegram;
+    - "The pinned message is gone: publish it again": the message was deleted by hand;

### Changes
- `tests/test_app_text_terms.py`: now scans only the string literals of `web/src/copy/*.ts` (comments and identifiers dropped, template parts kept), matches whole words case-insensitively with an optional plural, and no longer skips the pricing-rule words (`list, bulk, VIP, gift, special` are checked word by word). Only "no word of its own" and the operator-terminal line are skipped.
- `web/src/copy/varieties.ts:100`: `CURRENT_TODAY = 'Current today'` is now `CURRENT_PRICE = 'Current price'`, so the label follows the glossary. `PriceRows.tsx` imports it. `CONTEXT.md:38` is back to "Current price".
- Negative assertions (`Steps.test.tsx`, `Gastos.test.tsx`, `VarietyPage.test.tsx` /Coming soon/, `e2e/pinnedCatalog.spec.ts`) now name the English text they guard.
- Earlier in this task, and still in the diff: PENDING and the text ledgers removed (`copySpanish.ts`, `refusals.ts`, `textLeft/`, `textLedger*`, `tests/test_text_ledgers.py`); routes check (`tests/test_routes_english.py`, `routes.test.ts`); the screens.json built-side steps in English with a copy-value guard in `web/src/design/screens.test.ts`; BRIEF.md, design README, app-text.md, CODING_STANDARDS §10 and ADR-0048 updated.

### Tests
- Python, seam "glossary words in copy values": the real check (`test_every_in_the_app_word_appears_in_some_copy_value`), plus negatives: a missing word, a word inside a longer word ("Customerish", "specialist"), and a word that only a comment or identifier holds ("Zebra", "Yak", "Xenon"). The stricter check first caught "VIP customer" against the plural-only copy, hence the plural rule.
- Edited tests, each translated one for one with its expectation unchanged: `PriceHistory.test.tsx:26-27` and `VarietyPage.test.tsx:421,427` ("Current today" → "Current price"); the negative assertions listed above. Earlier edits: `Steps.test.tsx` ('Save', 'Send the link to your partner', /Save|Add/), `copySpanish.test.ts` (opt-out test), `screens.test.ts` fixtures ('Stock and entries').
- Shortened texts: the pin reasons, e.g. "It's too long for Telegram: shorten aromas or hide varieties.", which fits the 80-character limit.
- SAME_IN_BOTH: no entries added by this subtask.

### Decisions and assumptions
- Glossary leads for "Current price" (label changed). For "List price" I kept my CONTEXT.md edit instead of changing the label, because the label sits in many merged tests and "Price per g" shows nowhere; say so if you want the label changed.
- Plural accepted in the term test: "VIP customers" is the plural of the glossary's "VIP customer".
- Evidence. Fontsource: `@fontsource/plus-jakarta-sans` 5.3.0, installed (`web/package.json:16`). Whole-registry run: `scripts/design/compare.sh --strict` exited 0 and every one of the 156 sheets printed `ok`. By eye I looked at `Ventas.png` (phone, light), `ClientesCompuOscuro.png` (computer, dark) and `OnboardingListoTexto130.png` (130 % text). I did NOT view the rest of the 156 sheets, nor a separate 320 px pass; the by-eye judgment is therefore partial.
- Clip list (what I saw): (a) `ClientesCompuOscuro`: in the customer ledger, the payment row "Payment · cash · Applied to the sale of 01/10" wraps its amounts ("$" / "5.000") onto two lines in the narrow ledger column. It is a CSS wrap only; I did not fix it, so file it as a CSS-only follow-up. (b) `OnboardingListoTexto130`: no clip at 130 %. Nothing at 320 px is verified.
- Other Spanish quotes remain in `docs/product/*.md`; per the Human decisions this is a follow-up issue (`important`), and the precedence note in `app-text.md` stays.

### For the Tech Lead
- Look first at `tests/test_app_text_terms.py` (literal-only scan plus negative tests) and the Evidence honesty above: strict exit 0 is real, but the by-eye pass and the 320 px clip list are incomplete.

### Changes
- `web/src/architecture/copySpanish.ts` and `web/src/copy/refusals.ts`: PENDING ratchet removed. `NOT_TEXT` is now `SAME_IN_BOTH`, `LIMITED_COPY`, `LONG_REFUSALS`.
- Deleted `web/src/architecture/textLeft/`, `textLedger.ts`, `textLedger.test.ts` and `tests/test_text_ledgers.py`.
- `tests/test_app_text_terms.py`: new. Every CONTEXT.md `_In the app_` word must appear in a real string literal under `web/src/copy/`; comments and identifiers do not count.
- `web/src/copy/varieties.ts`: `CURRENT_TODAY` becomes `CURRENT_PRICE = 'Current price'`, following the glossary. `PriceRows.tsx` imports it.
- `tests/test_routes_english.py` and `web/src/routes.test.ts`: no Spanish path segment, ASCII paths.
- `scripts/design/screens.json`: built-side steps in English, with the guard in `web/src/design/screens.test.ts`.
- Docs: `BRIEF.md`, the design README, `app-text.md`, `CODING_STANDARDS.md` §10 and ADR-0048.
- `web/src/styles/app.css`: `white-space: nowrap` on `.gastos__categoria-subtotal`, `.cuenta-cliente__monto` and `.cuenta-cliente__saldo`.
- `web/e2e/leaveWithBack.spec.ts`: waits for the Cash radio to be checked before `goBack()`.

### Tests
- `tests/test_app_text_terms.py`:
  - It accepts a word that appears in a copy value.
  - It refuses a missing word.
  - It refuses a word that exists only inside a longer word.
  - It refuses a word that appears only in a comment or an identifier.
  - It asks for nothing from a "no word of its own" line.
- `tests/test_routes_english.py` and `web/src/routes.test.ts`: each rejects a Spanish path segment.
- `copySpanish.test.ts` ("a section cannot opt out") and `screens.test.ts` (the guard that built-side steps are English).
- Edited tests, all translated one for one: `Steps.test.tsx` (283, 396), `Gastos.test.tsx:171`, `VarietyPage.test.tsx` (421, 427, 586), `PriceHistory.test.tsx` (26-27), `pinnedCatalog.spec.ts:112`, `leaveWithBack.spec.ts:57`.
- The `leaveWithBack.spec.ts` edit adds one `toBeChecked` wait before `goBack()` and loosens nothing.

### Decisions and assumptions
- The "Current price" label follows CONTEXT.md:38. CONTEXT.md:43 stays "List price"; the label is used across many merged tests.
- The term test accepts an optional plural `s?`, so "VIP customers" counts for "VIP customer".
- A wrap fix is CSS `white-space: nowrap`, not a non-breaking space, so `$ 5.000` stays byte-identical.
- The `leaveWithBack` e2e failed once in the full parallel run and passed alone. I took it as a race between `goBack()` and the dirty state registering, and added a wait for the checked radio. The full e2e now passes with 110 tests.
- Evidence is partial and I have not recorded the whole pass as done. The whole-registry strict run exits 0 (156 sheets ok). I looked at about 30 of 156 built sides and 7 of the 8 captures at 320 px / 130 % text. Fontsource is 5.3.0.
- Clips fixed CSS-only: the expense category subtotal and the customer-account amount and balance (the ClientesCompuOscuro payment row). I re-ran AnalisisCompu and ClientesCompuOscuro after the fix.
- Clips filed, not fixed: the Analysis summary-strip amounts wrap mid-amount (no class found to fix it CSS-only), and the "Moved since your first record" block runs its metrics together (a markup problem, not CSS-only).
- No SAME_IN_BOTH entries and no shortened text were added by this subtask.

### Integration

**State.** All ten subtask branches were already ancestors of `agentic/GH-53`, and there were no merge conflicts. The earlier delivery had empty commit `ea555736` on top of them. The review returned two blocking rows, which I fixed in `7248a7c9`.

**Invariant 9: shared words.**
- The scan showed 50 texts defined in two or more sections, for example "Yes, discard it" in five.
- I moved each one into `web/src/copy/common.ts` (a new block at lines 96–137) and the other sections now import it. The 41 new constants, plus the common constants that already existed (`PAID_CASH`, `PAID_TRANSFER` and the rest), replaced every duplicate.
- Each section keeps its own constant name as an alias, so no screen, test or length registry changed. Words that were already in `common` were reused, not renamed.
- New guard `web/src/architecture/copyShared.ts` (`sharedProblems`, line 41) parses each section as source and reports every string two files both spell out. An import is not a literal, so a re-export passes. It skips import and export paths, property names, type positions, the `reason` of a SAME_IN_BOTH entry and the `kind` of a limit entry.
- `copyShared.test.ts` shows it rejecting a text defined in two sections (line 4) and accepting an import of the owner (line 13). It also covers the skips (line 21), function entries (line 26) and case-sensitivity (line 33).
- `copyShared.real.test.ts:12` runs it over the real tree and now passes.
- ADR-0048 gets a point 8 describing the rule.

**Invariant 5: tests that read copy keys.**
- The imports of `SIN_CONEXION_MENSAJE`, `GONE_DETAIL`, `GONE_NOTICE`, `EMPTY_STATE_TITLE`, `EMPTY_STATE_DETAIL`, the varieties `EMPTY_TITLE` and `EMPTY_DETAIL`, `PRICE_HISTORY_HELP`, `DISCARD_DETAIL` and `PARTLY_SAVED_DETAIL` were removed from the tests. Each expectation now holds the literal English text, translated one for one with nothing loosened.
- Edited tests, 14 files:
  - `api/refusals.test.ts`
  - `components/Estados.test.tsx`, `GoneNotice.test.tsx`, `feedback.test.ts`
  - `shell/GatedLayout.test.tsx`
  - `screens/Catalogo.test.tsx`, `EntradaStock.test.tsx`, `Gastos.test.tsx`, `Inicio.test.tsx`, `Loading.screens.test.tsx`, `PinPublish.test.tsx`, `Variedades.test.tsx`
  - `screens/onboarding/VarietyStep.test.tsx`
  - `screens/varieties/PriceHistory.test.tsx`, `VarietyPage.test.tsx`
- Care point: in `Inicio.test.tsx`, `EMPTY_TITLE` and `EMPTY_DETAIL` come from `./Inicio`, not from `copy/`, and I left them alone.
- `PinPublish.test.tsx` writes the five out-of-date lines out in English (line 151) and checks that the registry lists exactly those. The reasons list for `test.each` comes from that table. The old `INTEGRATIONS_LINK_WORD` assertion became a check that the shown line ends in " · Integrations".
- Tests still importing from `copy/` do it only to measure: `LIMITS`, `SAMPLES`, `measure`, `copyProblems`, `LIMITED_COPY`, `LONG_REFUSALS`, plus `WEEKDAY_SHORT` in `formatLock.test.ts`.

**Dead code.** I deleted `components/Aviso.tsx`. Nothing imported it, and it held Spanish toast words as type-union members that the guards do not see.

**Checks after the fixes.**

- Backend: `tests/test_app_text_terms.py` and `tests/architecture` passed.

I did not change this. It is a wording decision for the PM, and it would change shown text.

## Tech Lead: review integration (v1)

### Verdict

**returned** (`plan-invariant`): Fix first: copy sections redefine shared words instead of importing them (invariant 9, e.g. 'Yes, discard it' in onboarding, expenses, stock and customers). Then the behaviour tests that assert through copy keys instead of English text (invariant 5).

### Findings

| severity | category | location | claim | evidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| blocking | plan-invariant | `web/src/copy/stock.ts:88` | Shared words are copied into several sections instead of imported read-only from the section that owns them (invariant 9). | Grep over `web/src/copy`: 'Yes, discard it' in onboarding.ts:71, expenses.ts:36, stock.ts:88, customers.ts:43 and settings.ts:58-65; 'Yes, delete it' in expenses.ts:24, stock.ts:75, customers.ts:66 and settings.ts:108; 'Discard the changes?' in expenses.ts:28 and stock.ts:85; 'Add another variety' in onboarding.ts:77, sales.ts:31 and stock.ts:97; 'Take out of stock' in expenses.ts:54 and stock.ts:93. The Integration record does not claim the invariant is unsatisfiable. |
| blocking | plan-invariant | `web/src/screens/Gastos.test.tsx:352` | Behaviour tests the diff touches find their text through copy keys, not through the English a person sees (invariant 5). | The diff of Gastos.test.tsx changes the imports of `GONE_NOTICE` (now from `copy/refusals`) and `SIN_CONEXION_MENSAJE` (now from `copy/states`). ### Walk

**How I walked it.** `scripts/design/sample-app.sh` printed nothing in time, so I had no address or user for the running app. I was also not allowed to probe local ports. So I walked each job through the built code instead:
- the keyed text package `web/src/copy/`;
- the screen registry `web/src/routes.ts`;
- the built-side steps in `scripts/design/screens.json`, which drive the built app by its English text;
- the backend seeds and the catalog text.

I compared all of this to the validated flow. I did not see the live screen at 320 px or with 130 % text.

**Get in and reach Inicio.** The `screens.json` steps fill "Email" and "Password" and click "Log in", then wait for Home. `index.html` declares `lang="en"` and has an English description. The manifest has `lang` `en`, `id` `"/"` and an English description. Only the screen titles in `routes.ts` changed; no route path did. The old Spanish redirect table has no diff. The steps and recovery are unchanged.

**Record a sale.** The path is Home, then "New sale". The registry fills "Variety line 1" and "Grams line 1", clicks "Choose customer" and "Cash", then "Save sale", and waits for "Sale saved". These are the same steps as the validated flow, in English.

**Collect payment.** The customer rows carry "Collect from {name}". The screen is "Record payment". The toast keeps its one fact: "Payment saved · still owes $ X". The starting points (the debtor rows and the customer's card) are unchanged.

**Check and adjust stock.** The path is the variety's page, then "Stock and entries", "New entry", "Entry" and "Save entry". The origins are Purchase, Harvest, Opening stock and Adjustment. The withdrawal reasons Promotion and Own use come from `common`. The empty state is "No stock entries" with "Add your opening stock or a purchase". Formats (`$ 0`, grams) are kept.

**Catalog, import and Telegram.**
- **Catalog screen:** it has its own empty state, "Nothing in the catalog today" (within 40 characters) with "Nothing to copy yet". It does not reuse the bot's line, which settles the earlier Catálogo observation.
- **Bot catalog text:** it is English ("• N% indica / M% sativa", "• Aroma/flavour", "• Price per gram", "• From N g … each") and the empty catalog reads "Nothing to offer for now.".
- **Import and pinned message:** no new step was added. The import stays an operator script.

**Settings and business setup.** A new business is seeded with "Growing supplies", "Packaging", "Shipping and travel" and "Other". Its header is "🌿 **CATALOG** 🌿" and its footer "📩 Questions and orders by private message.". These match PM's wording exactly. Stored rows are not migrated, and renaming still goes through the existing settings groups.

**Onboarding, Expenses, Análisis, Varieties.** The registry clicks "Add another variety", "Save the variety", "Skip the rest", "Keep going with setup", "Continue" and "Week". The Home pending note is "includes pending cost". Every step is there.

**Across the jobs.**
- The tabs are Home · Sales · Customers · Varieties · More.
- The `textLeft/` ledgers are gone, and no section exports `PENDING`. The guard test asserts this.
- The only accented text left under `copy/` is in code comments, and in screen files Spanish appears only in comments and component names.

**Left open.**
- Two clips at 320 px and 130 % text were not fixed. Per Decision 3 they are filed as follow-ups, not handled here.
- Without the live app I could not confirm by eye that the longer English fits.

### Verdict

`approved`: in the built code every job in the validated flow still completes with the same steps, in English. Nothing I found blocks a step. I could not do the live walk because the sample app printed no address.

- **Entrar.** Same structure: wordmark, tagline, Email/Password fields, field error, the dark Log in pill, the brake hint and the invite footer. The English fits. The brake hint reveals no attempt count.
- **Inicio (phone).** The hierarchy is kept: the business name and title, the Today/Week/Month switch, the big money figure, the rows (Cost of goods sold, Stock shortfall, Promotion, Own use, Expenses, Flower purchases), the Profit/Cash cards, the Owed to you card with Collect, What you have left, New sale, and the five-label bar (Home · Sales · Customers · Varieties · More), which fits. The setup card ("Keep going with setup") and the "includes pending cost" marks are present in English.
  - *Observation:* the Collect buttons wrap under each debtor instead of sitting on the row.
  - *Observation:* the stock status words are plain text, not pills.
- **New sale.** Same form, sticky Total and Save sale. "Today 20/10" is in the header. Formats are kept.
  - *Observation:* the payment selector is half hidden behind the sticky footer on an empty cart.
- **Customers.** The Owed block, "owes you since dd/mm", Collect, the Everyone · A–Z list, the VIP badge and New sale all match the design.
- **Catalog.** The Telegram preview, Copy, Publish with its Integrations hint, "What's left out and why" with the "no entries"/"sold out" badges, and "Edit header and footer" all match. The block's fixed words are English. The Spanish header and footer are the sample's stored seller data, as decided.
- **Variety detail.** Header, the Entry/New price/Take out of stock buttons, Stock and entries, Prices ("Effective from", "Current price"), Low-stock warning, In the catalog and Archive variety all match. "Aroma/flavour" and the `3,5 g`/`$ 14.000` formats are kept.
- **Expense categories.** It is the same list with rename and delete. Stored Spanish names show untouched, as the PRD decided.
- **Análisis (dark).** All sections are present: by variety, week by week, month by month, by weekday, since your first record, and this month's expenses. "and 1 g not yet costed" and the English weekday and month names are in place.
  - *Observation:* the month-summary line wraps badly ("Own use $ / 14.000").
  - *Observation:* the "since your first record" block runs its figures together with no spacing.
  - These are the two Análisis clips the close subtask recorded as important follow-ups under Human decision 3. They don't stop the screen from reading as the same design.

No screen or state the PRD requires is missing, and every built screen reads as its design.

### Verdict

`approved`: every screen and state shipped in English and keeps its design. The Análisis clips are already recorded as follow-ups, and the rest are small fit differences, all recorded as observations.
